Then $P_N(c^{(N)})$ converges in expectation and almost surely to $$\\label{mag:finite-formula}\n \\mathcal F_\\mu\\left(\\sum_{j=1}^J\\gamma_j\\delta_{c_j}\\right)\n =\\sup_{\\mathbf m\\in((-1,1)\\cap\\mathbb Q)^J}\n   \\left\\{\\sum_{j=1}^J\\gamma_jc_jm_j\n    +\\inf_{p\\in\\mathcal P}\\bigl[S_{\\mathbf m}(p)+G_1(p)\\bigr]\\right\\}.$$ More generally, suppose the empirical field laws converge in the first-moment transport metric $W_1$ to a probability law $\\nu$ on $\\mathbb R$ with finite absolute first moment. Then the same modes of convergence hold, with limit $\\mathcal F_\\mu(\\nu)$, where $$\\label{mag:W1-formula}\n \\mathcal F_\\mu(\\nu)\n   =\\lim_{q\\to\\infty}\\mathcal F_\\mu(\\nu_q)$$ for any finite-support laws $\\nu_q$ with positive rational masses such that $W_1(\\nu_q,\\nu)\\to0$. This limit exists and is independent of the approximating sequence.",
      "explanation": "With deterministic external fields whose empirical laws converge in first-moment transport distance, the pressure converges to a limit given by a sup-inf formula over magnetizations and quantiles.",
      "fidelity": "PENDING",
      "hypotheses": [
        {
          "anchor": "stmt-mag-theorem",

      "id": "thm-magnetic-field",
      "kind": "theorem",
      "quantifiers": [
        {
          "anchor": "stmt-mag-theorem",

    {
      "conclusion": {
        "anchor": "stmt-mag-random-data",
        "exact_quote": "Let the spectrum and the field vector both be random. Conditional jointly on these data, assume that the interaction has the Haar orbit law with the prescribed spectrum. Let $\\mu$ be a fixed compactly supported spectral law and $\\nu$ a fixed field law with finite absolute first moment. If the spectral hypotheses of Equation <a href=\"#eq:spectral-assumptions\" data-reference-type=\"eqref\" data-reference=\"eq:spectral-assumptions\">[eq:spectral-assumptions]</a> and convergence of the empirical field laws to $\\nu$ in $W_1$ hold in probability, then $P_N(c^{(N)})\\to\\mathcal F_\\mu(\\nu)$ in probability. If both data convergences hold almost surely, the pressure convergence also holds almost surely. In either case, uniform integrability of the pressures implies convergence of their expectations.\n\nIndeed, condition on both data sets and apply the deterministic theorem along convergent data sequences. The subsequence argument and localized fourth-moment concentration in Proposition <a href=\"#gen:random\" data-reference-type=\"ref\" data-reference=\"gen:random\">31</a> apply unchanged, since the Haar Lipschitz constant is independent of the external field. <a id="pm-s2"></a>
## 2. Definitions, validity and bounds

- **pressure-PN** ([source](#eq:pressure)): Let $\Lambda_N=\mathop{\mathrm{diag}}(\lambda_{1,N},\ldots,\lambda_{N,N})$ be deterministic, and let $U_N$ be Haar distributed on the orthogonal group $O(N)$. Set $$P_N=\frac1N\log\left[ 2^{-N}\sum_{\sigma\in\{-1,1\}^N} \exp\left\{\frac12\sigma^{\mathsf T} U_N^{\mathsf T}\Lambda_NU_N\sigma\right\}\right]. \label{eq:pressure}$$ Temperature and coupling normalization are absorbed into $\Lambda_N$. Our spin prior is uniform probability, not counting measure.
- **spectral-assumptions** ([source](#eq:spectral-assumptions)): Write $\mu_N=N^{-1}\sum_i\delta_{\lambda_{i,N}}$. Throughout the main theorem, $\mu$ is a compactly supported probability measure on $\mathbb R$, with support edges $\lambda_-\le\lambda_+$, and $$\mu_N\Rightarrow\mu,\qquad \liminf_{N\to\infty}\min_i\lambda_{i,N}\ge\lambda_-,\qquad \limsup_{N\to\infty}\max_i\lambda_{i,N}\le\lambda_+. \label{eq:spectral-assumptions}$$ Weak convergence then forces convergence of both extreme eigenvalues to the corresponding edges. No density or nonzero-variance assumption is imposed on $\mu$.
- **edge-extended-R-transform** ([source](#eq:R)): For $x>0$, let $b(x)>\lambda_+$ be the unique solution, when it exists, of $$\int\frac{\mu(d\lambda)}{b(x)-\lambda}=x. \label{eq:resolvent}$$ If there is no such solution, put $b(x)=\lambda_+$. Define the edge-extended $R$-transform by $$R(x)=b(x)-\frac1x\quad(x>0),\qquad R(0)=\int\lambda\,\mu(d\lambda). \label{eq:R}$$ Only $0\le x\le1$ is needed in the final formula. The extension at the upper spectral edge is part of the definition; it is not an analytic continuation beyond the inverse-transform range.
- **overlap-distance-D** ([source](#eq:D)): $$D_p(r)=1-r-\int_0^1(p(s)-r)_+\,ds,\qquad 0\le r\le1. \label{eq:D}$$ This function is nonnegative and Lipschitz. Its value at $r=1$ is zero.
- **one-site-recursion** ([source](#eq:field-recursion)): $$F_{i-1}=T_{\zeta_i,h_i-h_{i-1}}F_i \quad (i=k-1,\ldots,0),\qquad \ell(h)=F_{-1}(0)-\frac{h_{k-1}}2. \label{eq:field-recursion}$$ Subdividing intervals on which $h$ is constant does not change the value. For $p\in\mathcal P$ and $0\le t\le1$, set $$\begin{aligned} S(p)&=\sup_{h\in\mathcal H} \left\{\ell(h)+\frac12\int_0^1p(s)h(s)\,ds\right\}, \label{eq:S}\\ G_t(p)&=\frac12\int_0^1tR(tD_p(r))\,dr, \qquad G_0(p)=0. \label{eq:G} \end{aligned}$$ The supremum is interpreted in the extended real sense. Both $S$ and the class of admissible fields are independent of the spectral measure.
- **field-supremum-S** ([source](#eq:S)): The one-site part of the formula is defined by a finite Gaussian recursion. Its auxiliary paths specify hierarchical Gaussian covariance levels. Let $\mathcal H$ consist of the nonnegative nondecreasing finite step functions on $(0,1)$. Describe $h\in\mathcal H$ by $k$ consecutive intervals of lengths $w_i>0$ and values $h_i$, for $0\le i<k$, and set $$\zeta_i=\sum_{l<i}w_l,\qquad \zeta_k=1, \qquad h_{-1}=0.$$ For $a,v\ge0$ and a function $f:\mathbb R\to\mathbb R$, write $$(T_{a,v}f)(z)= \begin{cases} a^{-1}\log\mathbb E\exp\{a f(z+\sqrt v\,g)\},&a>0,\\ \mathbb Ef(z+\sqrt v\,g),&a=0, \end{cases} \qquad g\sim N(0,1).$$ Starting from $F_{k-1}(z)=\log\cosh z$, define successively $$F_{i-1}=T_{\zeta_i,h_i-h_{i-1}}F_i \quad (i=k-1,\ldots,0),\qquad \ell(h)=F_{-1}(0)-\frac{h_{k-1}}2. \label{eq:field-recursion}$$ Subdividing intervals on which $h$ is constant does not change the value. For $p\in\mathcal P$ and $0\le t\le1$, set $$\begin{aligned} S(p)&=\sup_{h\in\mathcal H} \left\{\ell(h)+\frac12\int_0^1p(s)h(s)\,ds\right\}, \label{eq:S}\\ G_t(p)&=\frac12\int_0^1tR(tD_p(r))\,dr, \qquad G_0(p)=0. \label{eq:G} \end{aligned}$$ The supremum is interpreted in the extended real sense. Both $S$ and the class of admissible fields are independent of the spectral measure.
- **gram-array-and-GG** ([source](#arr:gg)): A Gram array is a symmetric array $(R_{lj})_{l,j\geq1}$ whose finite principal submatrices are positive semidefinite. It is weakly exchangeable if its law is invariant under finite permutations of its indices. For a finite vector $\mathbf R=(R^1,\ldots,R^m)$ of such arrays, let $\mathcal F_n$ be the sigma-field generated by all its entries with indices at most $n$. We say that $\mathbf R$ satisfies the joint Ghirlanda–Guerra identities if, for every $n\geq2$ and every bounded measurable $f$, $$\label{arr:gg} \mathbb E[f(\mathbf R_{1,n+1})\mid\mathcal F_n] =\frac1n\mathbb Ef(\mathbf R_{12}) +\frac1n\sum_{l=2}^n f(\mathbf R_{1l}).$$ The identities with any other distinguished index follow by exchangeability. In all applications the arrays are bounded and each diagonal is a deterministic constant.
- **perturbation** ([source](#rot:perturbation)): Set $e_N=N^{-1/16}$. For parameters $u_j,v_a\in[1,2]$, add $$\label{rot:perturbation} V_N(\sigma,\alpha) =Ne_N\sum_{a=1}^m v_a A^a_{11}(\sigma) +\sqrt N e_N\sum_{j\le N}2^{-j}u_jY_j(\sigma,\alpha)$$ to the Hamiltonian. Denote the resulting random pressure by $P_N^{\mathrm{enr}}(t,h,u,v)$. Gibbs brackets include the spin and cascade label, and $\mathbb E$ averages all disorder. Direct comparison for the first term and Gaussian comparison for the second give, uniformly in the displayed parameters, $$\label{rot:small-pressure} \left\|\mathbb EP_N^{\mathrm{enr}}-\mathbb EP_N^{\mathrm{enr},0}\right\| \le C e_N.$$ Here the superscript $0$ denotes omission of $V_N$, with the same prior and enrichment.
- **path-symbol** ([source](#rot:symbol)): By joint GG and synchronization, the group off-diagonal overlaps have simultaneous nondecreasing quantiles $p_a$, with $\sum_a p_a=p$. In particular each $p_a$ is constant on every plateau of $p$. We encode a diagonal and an off-diagonal path by $L=(d,l)$, and define its symbol by $$\label{rot:symbol} \widehat L(s)=d-sl(s)-\int_s^1 l(u)\,du,\qquad 0<s<1.$$ We choose right-continuous versions where appropriate. The symbol alone does not determine a path: replacing $(d,l)$ by $(d+c,l+c)$ leaves it unchanged. We therefore retain the root value $l(0+)$ as well. For the total overlap, $$\widehat Q(s)=1-sp(s)-\int_s^1p(u)\,du=D_p(p(s)).$$
- **group-resolvent-fractions** ([source](#rot:f)): Let $b_t(x)$ be the inverse resolvent for the finite law $\sum_a\rho_a\delta_{t\lambda_a}$, and set $$\label{rot:f} f_a(x)=\frac{\rho_a}{b_t(x)-t\lambda_a}\quad(x>0), \qquad f_a(0)=0.$$ For a finite alphabet of positive proportions, $b_t(x)>\max_a t\lambda_a$ for $x>0$. The functions $f_a$ are smooth jointly in $x,t$, including $x=0$: near zero, apply the inverse function theorem with $1/b_t$ as the inverse variable. Moreover $$\label{rot:f-derivative} f_a'(x)= \frac{\rho_a/(b_t(x)-t\lambda_a)^2} {\sum_b\rho_b/(b_t(x)-t\lambda_b)^2}, \qquad f_a'(0)=\rho_a,\qquad \sum_a f_a'(x)=1 .$$
- **cavity-field-path** ([source](#cav:field-path)): $$\label{cav:field-path} h_p(s)=\int_0^{p(s)}R'(D_p(r))\,dr.$$ This is a bounded nonnegative nondecreasing path. For $x>0$, $$R'(x)=\frac1{x^2}-\frac1{M_2(x)}\ge0, \qquad M_2(x)=\sum_a\frac{\rho_a}{(b(x)-\lambda_a)^2}\ge x^2,$$ and the derivative has its smooth nonnegative limit at zero. If $p$ is a finite step quantile, then $h_p\in\mathcal H$.
- **constrained-single-site-functional** ([source](#mag:constrained-functional)): For a deterministic vector $c^{(N)}=(c_1^{(N)},\ldots,c_N^{(N)})\in\mathbb R^N$, write $$\label{mag:pressure} P_N(c^{(N)})=\frac1N\log\left[ 2^{-N}\sum_{\sigma\in\{-1,1\}^N} \exp\left\{\frac12\sigma^{\mathsf T}U^{\mathsf T}\Lambda_NU\sigma +\sum_{r=1}^N c_r^{(N)}\sigma_r\right\}\right].$$ For $b\in\mathbb R$, let $\ell_b^{\mathrm{can}}(h)$ denote the single-site functional defined earlier, with initial position $b$ in place of zero; the subtraction $h_{k-1}/2$ is unchanged. For $s\in(-1,1)$ define $$\label{mag:constrained-functional} \ell^{[s]}(h)=\inf_{b\in\mathbb R} \{\ell_b^{\mathrm{can}}(h)-bs\}.$$ The bias $b$ is optimized to prescribe the mean spin $s$; the physical external field will contribute its linear term separately in the pressure formula. This single-site concave conjugate is used in generalized TAP theory [[ChenPanchenkoSubag2023](#ref-ChenPanchenkoSubag2023), 2.3, equations (2.21)–(2.23)]. Optimization over a conventional order parameter outside the Parisi order parameter also appears in Gaussian vector-spin formulas [[ChenConventional2024](#ref-ChenConventional2024), Theorem 1.1]. The covariance-level concavity and the constrained-block comparison needed for the present spectral model are proved in this section.
- **magnetization-supremum-S-m** ([source](#mag:Sm)): Given positive rational numbers $\gamma_1,\ldots,\gamma_J$ with sum one and $\mathbf m=(m_1,\ldots,m_J)\in((-1,1)\cap\mathbb Q)^J$, put $$\label{mag:Sm} \ell^{[\mathbf m]}(h)=\sum_{j=1}^J\gamma_j\ell^{[m_j]}(h), \qquad S_{\mathbf m}(p)=\sup_{h\in\mathcal H} \left\{\ell^{[\mathbf m]}(h)+\frac12\int_0^1p(s)h(s)\,ds\right\}.$$ The same covariance path $h$ is used in every field group.
- **wishart-pressure** ([source](#gen:wishart-pressure)): Fix $\alpha>0$, put $m_N=\lfloor\alpha N\rfloor$, and let $G_N$ be an $m_N$-by-$N$ matrix of independent standard real Gaussian variables. For $c\in\mathbb R$, define $$\label{gen:wishart-pressure} p_N(c)=\frac1N\log\left[ 2^{-N}\sum_{\sigma\in\{-1,1\}^N} \exp\left\{\frac{c}{2N}\\|G_N\sigma\\|_2^2\right\}\right].$$ This is the Gaussian-pattern Hopfield pressure for $J_N=cG_N^{\mathsf T}G_N/N$, with uniform spin probability and the full quadratic form, including its diagonal self-interactions.
- **wishart-spectral-law** ([source](#gen:wishart-law)): $$\label{gen:wishart-law} \begin{gathered} \mu_\alpha=(1-\alpha)_+\delta_0+ \frac{\sqrt{(b_\alpha-x)(x-a_\alpha)}}{2\pi x} \mathbf 1_{[a_\alpha,b_\alpha]}(x)\,dx,\\ \mu_{\alpha,c}=(x\mapsto cx)_\#\mu_\alpha. \end{gathered}$$ The displayed density is understood almost everywhere.

        {
          "anchor": "arr:gg",
          "exact_quote": "A Gram array is a symmetric array $(R_{lj})_{l,j\\geq1}$ whose finite principal submatrices are positive semidefinite. It is weakly exchangeable if its law is invariant under finite permutations of its indices. For a finite vector $\\mathbf R=(R^1,\\ldots,R^m)$ of such arrays, let $\\mathcal F_n$ be the sigma-field generated by all its entries with indices at most $n$. We say that $\\mathbf R$ satisfies the joint Ghirlanda–Guerra identities if, for every $n\\geq2$ and every bounded measurable $f$, $$\\label{arr:gg}\n \\mathbb E[f(\\mathbf R_{1,n+1})\\mid\\mathcal F_n]\n =\\frac1n\\mathbb Ef(\\mathbf R_{12})\n   +\\frac1n\\sum_{l=2}^n f(\\mathbf R_{1l}).$$ The identities with any other distinguished index follow by exchangeability. In all applications the arrays are bounded and each diagonal is a deterministic constant.",
          "source_id": "PM-234.1"
        }
      ],
      "exact_definition": "A Gram array is a symmetric array $(R_{lj})_{l,j\\geq1}$ whose finite principal submatrices are positive semidefinite. It is weakly exchangeable if its law is invariant under finite permutations of its indices. For a finite vector $\\mathbf R=(R^1,\\ldots,R^m)$ of such arrays, let $\\mathcal F_n$ be the sigma-field generated by all its entries with indices at most $n$. We say that $\\mathbf R$ satisfies the joint Ghirlanda–Guerra identities if, for every $n\\geq2$ and every bounded measurable $f$, $$\\label{arr:gg}\n \\mathbb E[f(\\mathbf R_{1,n+1})\\mid\\mathcal F_n]\n =\\frac1n\\mathbb Ef(\\mathbf R_{12})\n   +\\frac1n\\sum_{l=2}^n f(\\mathbf R_{1l}).$$ The identities with any other distinguished index follow by exchangeability. In all applications the arrays are bounded and each diagonal is a deterministic constant.",

        {
          "anchor": "rot:symbol",
          "exact_quote": "By joint GG and synchronization, the group off-diagonal overlaps have simultaneous nondecreasing quantiles $p_a$, with $\\sum_a p_a=p$. In particular each $p_a$ is constant on every plateau of $p$. We encode a diagonal and an off-diagonal path by $L=(d,l)$, and define its symbol by $$\\label{rot:symbol}\n \\widehat L(s)=d-sl(s)-\\int_s^1 l(u)\\,du,\\qquad 0<s<1.$$ We choose right-continuous versions where appropriate. The symbol alone does not determine a path: replacing $(d,l)$ by $(d+c,l+c)$ leaves it unchanged. We therefore retain the root value $l(0+)$ as well. For the total overlap, $$\\widehat Q(s)=1-sp(s)-\\int_s^1p(u)\\,du=D_p(p(s)).$$",
          "source_id": "PM-234.1"
        }
      ],
      "exact_definition": "By joint GG and synchronization, the group off-diagonal overlaps have simultaneous nondecreasing quantiles $p_a$, with $\\sum_a p_a=p$. The symbol alone does not determine a path: replacing $(d,l)$ by $(d+c,l+c)$ leaves it unchanged. We therefore retain the root value $l(0+)$ as well. For the total overlap, $$\\widehat Q(s)=1-sp(s)-\\int_s^1p(u)\\,du=D_p(p(s)).$$",
      "interpretation": "Symbol of a diagonal-plus-off-diagonal path, turning the replica product into ordinary multiplication.",

          "anchor": "rot:f",
          "exact_quote": "Let $b_t(x)$ be the inverse resolvent for the finite law $\\sum_a\\rho_a\\delta_{t\\lambda_a}$, and set $$\\label{rot:f}\n f_a(x)=\\frac{\\rho_a}{b_t(x)-t\\lambda_a}\\quad(x>0),\n \\qquad f_a(0)=0.$$ For a finite alphabet of positive proportions, $b_t(x)>\\max_a t\\lambda_a$ for $x>0$. The functions $f_a$ are smooth jointly in $x,t$, including $x=0$: near zero, apply the inverse function theorem with $1/b_t$ as the inverse variable. Moreover $$\\label{rot:f-derivative}\n f_a'(x)=\n \\frac{\\rho_a/(b_t(x)-t\\lambda_a)^2}\n      {\\sum_b\\rho_b/(b_t(x)-t\\lambda_b)^2},\n \\qquad\n f_a'(0)=\\rho_a,\\qquad \\sum_a f_a'(x)=1 .$$",

        }

For $s\\in(-1,1)$ define $$\\label{mag:constrained-functional}\n \\ell^{[s]}(h)=\\inf_{b\\in\\mathbb R}\n       \\{\\ell_b^{\\mathrm{can}}(h)-bs\\}.$$ The bias $b$ is optimized to prescribe the mean spin $s$; the physical external field will contribute its linear term separately in the pressure formula. |---|---|---|---|---|
| q-lipschitz-field-functional | Lipschitz constant of ell in the integral distance | ≤ | $\frac12$ | fixed interval weights |
| q-perturbation-scale | perturbation scale e_N | = | $N^{-1/16}$ | dimension N |
| q-D-at-one | D_p(1) | = | $0$ | all p in P |
| q-S-at-unit-quantile | S(p) at the extremal quantile | = | $+\infty$ | d equal to zero |
| q-energy-bound | interaction energy per spin | ≤ | $L/2$ | t in [0,1], p in P |
| q-wishart-lower-edge | Marchenko-Pastur lower edge | = | $(1-\sqrt\alpha)^2$ | alpha>0 |
| q-wishart-upper-edge | Marchenko-Pastur upper edge | = | $(1+\sqrt\alpha)^2$ | alpha>0 |

<a id="pm-s3"></a>
## 3. Proof roadmap

- **step-gg-invariance** [claim](#stmt-arr-invariance) / [proof](#proof-stmt-arr-invariance): Empirical means of exchangeable variables, derivative iteration with extra replicas, analyticity -> invariance identity
- **step-duplication** [claim](#stmt-arr-duplication) / [proof](#proof-stmt-arr-duplication): Contradiction using the invariance identity -> duplication principle
- **step-array-structure** [claim](#stmt-arr-structure) / [proof](#proof-stmt-arr-structure): Barycenter contradiction for ultrametricity, coordinate-and-sum argument for synchronization -> ordered quantile representation
- **step-marked-cascade** [claim](#stmt-arr-rpc) / [proof](#proof-stmt-arr-rpc): Poisson mapping of edge points with telescoping multipliers -> marked cascade recursion
- **step-cascade-gg** [claim](#stmt-arr-rpc-gg) / [proof](#proof-stmt-arr-rpc-gg): Gaussian integration by parts on marks, induction on sample size for uniqueness -> cascade satisfies identities
- **step-finite-replica** [claim](#stmt-arr-finite-replica) / [proof](#proof-stmt-arr-finite-replica): Polynomial approximation of the logarithm and inverse powers via extra replicas -> limit passage for finite replicas
- **step-gaussian-differentiation** [claim](#stmt-arr-gaussian-calculus) / [proof](#proof-stmt-arr-gaussian-calculus): Gaussian integration by parts on finite priors, then approximation -> differentiation formulas
- **step-field-derivative** [claim](#stmt-fld-derivative) / [proof](#proof-stmt-fld-derivative): Martingale of conditional means and Gaussian differentiation of the one-site cascade -> gradient of ell
- **step-mixed-monotone** [claim](#stmt-fld-mixed) / [proof](#proof-stmt-fld-mixed): Stochastic monotonicity of folded tilted transitions -> nonpositive mixed derivatives
- **step-radial-monotone** [claim](#stmt-fld-radial) / [proof](#proof-stmt-fld-radial): Backward induction on conditional spin means under scaling -> radial monotonicity
- **step-field-concavity** [claim](#stmt-fld-concavity) / [proof](#proof-stmt-fld-concavity): Hessian with nonpositive off-diagonal entries and nonnegative scaled row sums is negative semidefinite -> concavity and supporting inequality
- **step-domain-of-S** [claim](#stmt-fld-domain) / [proof](#proof-stmt-fld-domain): Majorant of log cosh and iteration through the field recursion -> bounds on S
- **step-concentration** [claim](#stmt-rot-concentration) / [proof](#proof-stmt-rot-concentration): Poincare inequality on the orthogonal group and marked cascade decomposition -> variance and fourth-moment bounds
- **step-perturbation-gg** [claim](#stmt-rot-gg) / [proof](#proof-stmt-rot-gg): Convex secants of the perturbed pressure with concentration -> identities for limits
- **step-spectral-ward** [claim](#stmt-rot-ward) / [proof](#proof-stmt-rot-ward): Differentiation under plane rotations -> Ward identities
- **step-replica-product** [claim](#stmt-rot-product) / [proof](#proof-stmt-rot-product): Conditional laws on plateaus and Stieltjes calculus -> path product
- **step-projected-paths** [claim](#stmt-rot-paths) / [proof](#proof-stmt-rot-paths): Solve symbol equations via the resolvent -> group paths from total overlap
- **step-energy-tail-order** [claim](#stmt-up-energy) / [proof](#proof-stmt-up-energy): Convexity from Cauchy-Schwarz and tail-order argument -> energy ordering
- **step-finite-upper-bound** [claim](#stmt-up-bound) / [proof](#proof-stmt-up-bound): Contact argument with a fixed trial quantile -> upper bound
- **step-cavity-coupling** [claim](#stmt-cav-coupling) / [proof](#proof-stmt-cav-coupling): Orthonormal completion of the cavity coordinate map -> coupled interactions
- **step-cavity-comparison** [claim](#stmt-cav-comparison) / [proof](#proof-stmt-cav-comparison): Gaussian interpolation with a penalty -> increment lower bound
- **step-quadratic-tilt** [claim](#stmt-cav-quadratic) / [proof](#proof-stmt-cav-quadratic): Backward Gaussian integration with determinant factorization -> tilted cascade law
- **step-cutoff-removal** [claim](#stmt-cav-cutoffs) / [proof](#proof-stmt-cav-cutoffs): Jensen inequality and Gaussian moment bounds -> uniform moment bound
- **step-cavity-evaluation** [claim](#stmt-cav-evaluation) / [proof](#proof-stmt-cav-evaluation): Schur complement and Fubini evaluation -> evaluated increment
- **step-self-consistency** [claim](#stmt-cav-self-consistency) / [proof](#proof-stmt-cav-self-consistency): Site symmetry tests and compactness -> field attains supremum
- **step-finite-lower-bound** [claim](#stmt-cav-lower) / [proof](#proof-stmt-cav-lower): Telescoping increments over parameter averages -> lower bound
- **step-R-continuity** [claim](#stmt-gen-r-continuity) / [proof](#proof-stmt-gen-r-continuity): Compactness argument on the inverse resolvent -> uniform convergence of the transform
- **step-count-comparison** [claim](#stmt-gen-counts) / [proof](#proof-stmt-gen-counts): Convexity and rotation within eigenspaces -> multiplicity bound
- **step-general-approximation** [claim](#stmt-gen-approximation) / [proof](#proof-stmt-gen-approximation): Rational counts, binning and coordinatewise sandwich -> expected pressure limit
- **step-random-spectra** [claim](#stmt-gen-random) / [proof](#proof-stmt-gen-random): Conditioning on eigenvalues with localized concentration -> random spectrum limit
- **step-ground-state** [claim](#stmt-gen-ground-state) / [proof](#proof-stmt-gen-ground-state): Sandwich between scaled pressure and maximum energy -> ground-state limit
- **step-gaussian-patterns** [claim](#stmt-gen-wishart) / [proof](#proof-stmt-gen-wishart): Marchenko-Pastur law, edge control and zero-temperature squeeze -> Gaussian pattern limits
- **step-constrained-concavity** [claim](#stmt-mag-concavity) / [proof](#proof-stmt-mag-concavity): Envelope derivative and comparison for inverse-coordinate equations -> constrained concavity
- **step-block-replacement** [claim](#stmt-mag-replacement) / [proof](#proof-stmt-mag-replacement): Relative entropy chain rule and independent-site concentration -> uniform block replacement
- **step-magnetic-field** [claim](#stmt-mag-theorem) / [proof](#stmt-mag-theorem): Slice upper bound, block lower bound, then field approximation through the Lipschitz estimate -> pressure limit with field
- **step-random-data** [claim](#stmt-mag-random-data) / [proof](#stmt-mag-random-data): Conditioning and subsequence argument -> random data extension

<a id="pm-s4"></a>
## 4. Engineering Triptych

### Panel 1 - epistemic contract

- **inv-pressure-limit**: Under weak spectral convergence to a compactly supported law with extreme eigenvalues converging to the support edges, the pressure converges in expectation and almost surely to the variational value given by an infimum over trial overlap quantiles.
- **inv-mixed-monotone**: On the strict covariance cone the expected squared conditional magnetizations have nonpositive mixed derivatives in the covariance levels, which feeds the concavity of the one-site functional.
- **inv-domain-of-S**: The Legendre-type functional S is finite for every trial quantile except the extremal one, with an explicit upper bound in terms of the mean gap from one.

### Panel 2 - negative knowledge

- **anti-sparse-interaction**: Apply the pressure formula to sparse or non-rotationally-invariant interaction matrices. -> The theorem is proved only when the eigenvectors are Haar orthogonal; nothing is asserted for other eigenvector structures.
- **anti-unbounded-support**: Replace the edge-convergence hypothesis by the weaker statement that eigenvalues lie in the support of an unbounded limiting law. -> The source gives an explicit sequence for which the pressure has no limit.
- **anti-extremal-quantile**: Evaluate the variational functional at the trial quantile equal to one almost everywhere as if S were finite. -> S is infinite at that quantile, so the bound is vacuous there.

### Panel 3 - reference implementation and oracle

Applicability: `not_applicable`. The main result is a limit equal to an infimum over a function space of trial overlap quantiles, each evaluated through a supremum over covariance paths; the proofs are existence and comparison arguments with no finite terminating procedure that computes the value.

<a id="pm-s5"></a>
## 5. Domain engineering contracts

| domain | applies | strength | maturity | basis | why |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| software | False | none | not_applicable | inferred | The paper proves a limiting pressure formula and gives no procedure with explicit steps that could be shipped as a library routine. |
| games_simulation | False | none | not_applicable | inferred | The result concerns thermodynamic limits of an Ising system and offers no simulation scheme, game mechanic or procedural-generation rule. |
| tools | False | none | not_applicable | inferred | No tool or checker can be built from the statements alone, because the limit is defined through an infimum over trial quantiles and a supremum over fields. |
| algorithms | False | none | not_applicable | inferred | The main theorem is non-constructive: it identifies a limit and gives no finite procedure and no running-time guarantee. |
| data_structures | False | none | not_applicable | inferred | The paper introduces no storage layout or indexed structure; its objects (overlap arrays, cascades, quantile paths) are analytic devices in a proof. |
| hardware_accelerators | False | none | not_applicable | inferred | No arithmetic kernel, memory-access pattern or parallel scheme appears; the work is purely a limit theorem. |
| crypto_security | False | none | not_applicable | inferred | There is no hardness assumption, primitive or security reduction; the spin system is a statistical-mechanics model. |
| machine_learning | True | moderate | adaptable | inferred | Gaussian-pattern Hopfield interactions are covered, so the limiting free energy of such associative-memory-type quadratic Ising models can be read off from the formula. It is a reference value to compare against, not a training method. |
| optimisation_operations_research | True | moderate | adaptable | inferred | Taking the temperature to zero gives the limiting maximum energy per spin of a binary quadratic form with a Haar-rotated spectrum, which can serve as a benchmark for optimisation heuristics on such instances. |
| distributed_systems | False | none | not_applicable | inferred | No coordination, communication or fault model appears anywhere in the paper. |
| other (statistical physics and random matrix theory) | True | strong | inspirational | inferred | The result is a rigorous replica-type formula for mean-field spin glasses with orthogonally invariant couplings, expressed through the edge-extended R-transform of the limiting spectral law. |

<a id="pm-s6"></a>
## 6. Applicability limits and search

- **limit-spectral-hypotheses** (hypothesis): The pressure limit is proved only when the empirical spectral measures converge weakly to a compactly supported law and the extreme eigenvalues converge to the support edges, so no asymptotic outliers occur. Blocked use: ['Applying the formula to spectra with outlier eigenvalues', 'Applying the formula to spectra with unbounded limiting support']
- **limit-unbounded-support-counterexample** (counterexample_scope): Weak convergence with all eigenvalues in the support of a possibly unbounded law does not suffice; an explicit sequence has no pressure limit. Blocked use: ['Replacing the edge-convergence hypothesis by membership in the support', 'Using the formula for heavy-tailed spectra']
- **limit-haar-orthogonal** (hypothesis): The result is stated for Haar distributed orthogonal eigenvector matrices; nothing is claimed for sparse or otherwise structured interactions. Blocked use: ['Applying the formula to sparse adjacency matrices', 'Applying it to interactions whose eigenvectors are not Haar distributed']
- **limit-nonconstructive-variational** (nonconstructive): The limit is an infimum over trial quantiles of a functional containing a supremum over fields, and the order of infimum and supremum is essential; no finite procedure or running time is given. Blocked use: ['Claiming polynomial-time guarantees for heuristics', 'Exchanging the infimum and the supremum', 'Certifying the optimum of one finite instance']
- **limit-domain-of-S** (edge_case): The field supremum is infinite at the extremal quantile equal to one almost everywhere, so the variational value is only meaningful through trials with positive mean gap. Blocked use: ['Evaluating the functional at the unit quantile as a finite value']
- **limit-finite-alphabet-stages** (formal_scope): The upper and lower bounds are proved first for finite spectral alphabets, with rational proportions for the lower bound, and extended afterwards by approximation; intermediate constants depend on the alphabet. Blocked use: ['Using finite-alphabet constants uniformly across spectral approximations']
- **limit-magnetic-field-regime** (hypothesis): The external-field formula needs deterministic fields whose empirical laws converge in the first-moment transport distance to a law with finite absolute first moment, together with the spectral hypotheses of the main theorem. Blocked use: ['Applying it to fields without first-moment control', 'Applying it to fields that depend on the interaction']

<a id="pm-formal"></a>
### Formal statement (Lean 4)

Corpus status: formalized in the source corpus (toolchain `leanprover/lean4:v4.34.1`, Mathlib `d13f23b723b8a846827a245b89c10fc7d3f11612`). Not re-checked here; the statements give the exact quantifiers and hypotheses, not a proof.

Scope note of family 234 (`lean/docs/234.md`, verbatim):

> The following describes the scope of the Lean formalization related to the following accompanying paper(s):
>
> - [All-temperature pressure for orthogonally invariant Ising spin glasses](../../preprints/All-temperature-pressure-for-orthogonally-invariant-Ising-spin-glasses-September-25-2026/paper.pdf)
>
> ## Scope
>
> The formalization gives variational limits for orthogonally invariant Ising spin glasses whose eigenvectors have Haar law and whose empirical eigenvalue distributions converge to a compactly supported law with the stated control of extreme eigenvalues. The pressure converges both in expectation and almost surely to an explicit functional of the limiting spectral law. A version with external fields assumes convergence of their empirical laws in Wasserstein distance and gives the corresponding magnetic-field functional.
>
> The formalization also covers every positive temperature, a ground-state limit, random spectral data with the stated conditional Haar law, and Gaussian-pattern specializations. These hypotheses identify the invariant models to which the limits apply.

import Mathlib

namespace OAI

/-! All-temperature invariant Ising pressure and its magnetic, random-spectral,
ground-state, and Gaussian-pattern extensions. -/

noncomputable section
open MeasureTheory ProbabilityTheory Filter Set
open scoped BigOperators Topology Matrix Classical ENNReal
universe u
namespace InvariantIsing

abbrev Spin (N : ℕ) := Fin N → Bool

def spinValue (b : Bool) : ℝ := if b then 1 else -1

def logPartition {X : Type*} [Fintype X] (H : X → ℝ) : ℝ :=
  Real.log ((Fintype.card X : ℝ)⁻¹ * ∑ x, Real.exp (H x))

def fieldEnergy {N : ℕ} (c : Fin N → ℝ) (σ : Spin N) : ℝ :=
  ∑ i, c i * spinValue (σ i)

abbrev Rotation (N : ℕ) :=
  EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin N) ≃ₗᵢ[ℝ] EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin N)

abbrev Orthogonal (N : ℕ) := Matrix.orthogonalGroup (Fin N) ℝ

def spinVector {N : ℕ} (σ : Spin N) : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin N) :=
  WithLp.toLp 2 (fun i => spinValue (σ i))

def rotatedEnergy {N : ℕ} (eig : Fin N → ℝ) (U : Rotation N) (σ : Spin N) : ℝ :=
  (1 / 2 : ℝ) * ∑ i, eig i * (U (spinVector σ) i) ^ 2

def rotatedPressure {N : ℕ} (eig : Fin N → ℝ) (U : Rotation N)
    (c : Fin N → ℝ) : ℝ :=
  (N : ℝ)⁻¹ * logPartition (fun σ => rotatedEnergy eig U σ + fieldEnergy c σ)

run_cmd Lean.modifyEnv fun env => Lean.Meta.auxLemmasExt.setState env {}

lemma orthogonal_mulVec_norm_sq {N : ℕ} (U : Orthogonal N)
    (v : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin N)) :
    ‖WithLp.toLp 2 ((U : Matrix (Fin N) (Fin N) ℝ) *ᵥ v.ofLp)‖ ^ 2 = ‖v‖ ^ 2 := by
  have hU : (U : Matrix (Fin N) (Fin N) ℝ)ᵀ *
      (U : Matrix (Fin N) (Fin N) ℝ) = 1 :=
    (Matrix.mem_orthogonalGroup_iff' (Fin N) ℝ).mp U.property
  rw [EuclideanSpace.real_norm_sq_eq, EuclideanSpace.real_norm_sq_eq]
  simp only [pow_two]
  change ((U : Matrix (Fin N) (Fin N) ℝ) *ᵥ v.ofLp) ⬝ᵥ
      ((U : Matrix (Fin N) (Fin N) ℝ) *ᵥ v.ofLp) = v.ofLp ⬝ᵥ v.ofLp
  rw [← Matrix.dotProduct_transpose_mulVec, Matrix.mulVec_mulVec, hU,
    Matrix.one_mulVec]

def matrixRotation {N : ℕ} (U : Orthogonal N) : Rotation N :=
  { ((WithLp.linearEquiv 2 ℝ (Fin N → ℝ)).trans
      (Matrix.UnitaryGroup.toLinearEquiv U)).trans
      (WithLp.linearEquiv 2 ℝ (Fin N → ℝ)).symm with
    norm_map' := fun v => (sq_eq_sq₀ (norm_nonneg _) (norm_nonneg _)).mp
      (orthogonal_mulVec_norm_sq U v) }

structure OverlapPath where
  val : ℝ → ℝ
  monotone : Monotone val
  nonneg : ∀ s, 0 ≤ val s
  le_one : ∀ s, val s ≤ 1

instance : CoeFun OverlapPath (fun _ => ℝ → ℝ) := ⟨OverlapPath.val⟩

def pathMeasure : Measure ℝ := volume.restrict (Ioo (0 : ℝ) 1)

def deficit (p : OverlapPath) (r : ℝ) : ℝ :=
  1 - r - ∫ s, max (p s - r) 0 ∂pathMeasure

run_cmd Lean.modifyEnv fun env => Lean.Meta.auxLemmasExt.setState env {}

def gaussianOperator (a v : ℝ) (f : ℝ → ℝ) (z : ℝ) : ℝ :=
  if a = 0 then ∫ g, f (z + Real.sqrt v * g) ∂gaussianReal 0 1
  else a⁻¹ * Real.log (∫ g, Real.exp (a * f (z + Real.sqrt v * g)) ∂gaussianReal 0 1)

structure FieldStep where
  depth : ℕ
  cut : Fin (depth + 2) → ℝ
  ordered_cut : StrictMono cut
  first : cut 0 = 0
  last : cut (Fin.last (depth + 1)) = 1
  height : Fin (depth + 1) → ℝ
  nonneg : ∀ i, 0 ≤ height i
  ordered_height : Monotone height

def fieldFunction (h : FieldStep) (s : ℝ) : ℝ :=
  ∑ i : Fin (h.depth + 1),
    if h.cut i.castSucc < s ∧ s < h.cut i.succ then h.height i else 0

def fieldIncrement (h : FieldStep) (i : Fin (h.depth + 1)) : ℝ × ℝ :=
  (h.cut i.castSucc, h.height i -
    if hi : i.val = 0 then 0 else h.height ⟨i.val - 1, by omega⟩)

def fieldValue (h : FieldStep) (b : ℝ) : ℝ :=
  ((List.ofFn (fieldIncrement h)).foldr
    (fun av f => gaussianOperator av.1 av.2 f) (fun z => Real.log (Real.cosh z))) b -
    h.height (Fin.last h.depth) / 2

def fieldPairing (p : OverlapPath) (h : FieldStep) : ℝ :=
  ∫ s, p s * fieldFunction h s ∂pathMeasure

def entropyFunctional (p : OverlapPath) : EReal :=
  ⨆ h : FieldStep, ((fieldValue h 0 + fieldPairing p h / 2 : ℝ) : EReal)

def spectralFunctional (R : ℝ → ℝ) (p : OverlapPath) : ℝ :=
  (1 / 2 : ℝ) * ∫ r, R (deficit p r) ∂pathMeasure

def variationalFunctional (R : ℝ → ℝ) : EReal :=
  ⨅ p : OverlapPath, entropyFunctional p + (spectralFunctional R p : EReal)

def measureResolvent (μ : Measure ℝ) (b : ℝ) : ℝ := ∫ y, 1 / (b - y) ∂μ

def measureInverse (μ : Measure ℝ) (e x : ℝ) : ℝ :=
  if h : ∃ b, e < b ∧ measureResolvent μ b = x then h.choose else e

def measureR (μ : Measure ℝ) (e x : ℝ) : ℝ :=
  if 0 < x then measureInverse μ e x - 1 / x else ∫ y, y ∂μ

def finiteSpectralMeasure {ι : Type*} (ρ eig : ι → ℝ) : Measure ℝ :=
  Measure.sum fun a => ENNReal.ofReal (ρ a) • Measure.dirac (eig a)

theorem finiteSpectralMeasure_probability {ι : Type*} [Fintype ι] (ρ eig : ι → ℝ)
    (hρ : ∀ a, 0 ≤ ρ a) (hsum : ∑ a, ρ a = 1) :
    IsProbabilityMeasure (finiteSpectralMeasure ρ eig) := by
  apply HasSum.isProbabilityMeasure_sum_dirac hρ
  simpa only [hsum] using hasSum_fintype ρ

run_cmd Lean.modifyEnv fun env => Lean.Meta.auxLemmasExt.setState env {}

lemma empiricalSpectralWeight_sum {N : ℕ} (hN : 0 < N) :
    ∑ _i : Fin N, (1 : ℝ)/N=1 := by
  simp only [Finset.sum_const, Finset.card_univ, Fintype.card_fin, nsmul_eq_mul]
  field_simp

def empiricalSpectralLaw {N : ℕ} (hN : 0 < N) (eig : Fin N → ℝ) : ProbabilityMeasure ℝ :=
  ⟨finiteSpectralMeasure (fun _ => (1 : ℝ)/N) eig,
    finiteSpectralMeasure_probability _ _ (fun _ => by positivity) (empiricalSpectralWeight_sum hN)⟩

def spectralMinimum {n : ℕ} (eig : Fin (n+1) → ℝ) : ℝ :=
  (Finset.univ.image eig).min' (Finset.image_nonempty.mpr Finset.univ_nonempty)

def spectralMaximum {n : ℕ} (eig : Fin (n+1) → ℝ) : ℝ :=
  (Finset.univ.image eig).max' (Finset.image_nonempty.mpr Finset.univ_nonempty)

def magneticBiasObjective (h : FieldStep) (s b : ℝ) : ℝ := fieldValue h b - b * s

def constrainedFieldValue (h : FieldStep) (s : ℝ) : ℝ :=
  sInf (range (magneticBiasObjective h s))

def magneticGroupValue {A : Type*} [Fintype A] (γ m : A → ℝ) (h : FieldStep) : ℝ :=
  ∑ a, γ a * constrainedFieldValue h (m a)

run_cmd Lean.modifyEnv fun env => Lean.Meta.auxLemmasExt.setState env {}

def magneticEntropyFunctional {A : Type*} [Fintype A] (γ m : A → ℝ)
    (p : OverlapPath) : EReal :=
  ⨆ h : FieldStep, ((magneticGroupValue γ m h + fieldPairing p h / 2 : ℝ) : EReal)

def magneticVariationalFunctional {A : Type*} [Fintype A] (R : ℝ → ℝ) (γ m : A → ℝ) : EReal :=
  ⨅ p : OverlapPath, magneticEntropyFunctional γ m p + (spectralFunctional R p : EReal)

def RationalMagnetization (A : Type*) := {m : A → ℚ // ∀ a, |(m a : ℝ)| < 1}

def finiteMagneticFunctional {A : Type*} [Fintype A] (R : ℝ → ℝ) (γ c : A → ℝ) : EReal :=
  ⨆ m : RationalMagnetization A,
    ((∑ a, γ a * c a * (m.val a : ℝ) : ℝ) : EReal) +
      magneticVariationalFunctional R γ (fun a => (m.val a : ℝ))

def finiteIndexWeight {Ω A : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Ω]
    (P : Measure Ω) (g : Ω → A) (a : A) : ℝ := P.real (g ⁻¹' {a})

def simpleFieldIndex {Ω : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Ω] (s : SimpleFunc Ω ℝ) (x : Ω) : s.range :=
  ⟨s x,s.mem_range_self x⟩

def simpleFieldWeight {Ω : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Ω] (P : Measure Ω) (s : SimpleFunc Ω ℝ) :=
  finiteIndexWeight P (simpleFieldIndex s)

abbrev SimpleFieldPositive {Ω : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Ω] (P : Measure Ω) (s : SimpleFunc Ω ℝ) :=
  {i : s.range // 0 < simpleFieldWeight P s i}

def simpleFieldValue {Ω : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Ω]
    (P : Measure Ω) [IsProbabilityMeasure P] (s : SimpleFunc Ω ℝ) (R : ℝ → ℝ) : ℝ :=
  (finiteMagneticFunctional R (fun i : SimpleFieldPositive P s => simpleFieldWeight P s i)
    (fun i => i.val.val)).toReal

def fieldSimpleApproximation (n : ℕ) : SimpleFunc ℝ ℝ :=
  SimpleFunc.approxOn id measurable_id (range (id : ℝ → ℝ) ∪ {0}) 0 (by simp) n

def magneticFieldFunctional (spectral : Measure ℝ) (edge : ℝ) (field : ProbabilityMeasure ℝ) : ℝ :=
  limUnder atTop (fun n => simpleFieldValue (field : Measure ℝ) (fieldSimpleApproximation n)
    (measureR spectral edge))

structure FieldLawCoupling (μ ν : ProbabilityMeasure ℝ) where
  law : ProbabilityMeasure (ℝ × ℝ)
  fst : (law : Measure (ℝ × ℝ)).map Prod.fst = (μ : Measure ℝ)
  snd : (law : Measure (ℝ × ℝ)).map Prod.snd = (ν : Measure ℝ)

def FieldLawCoupling.cost {μ ν : ProbabilityMeasure ℝ} (p : FieldLawCoupling μ ν) : ℝ≥0∞ :=
  ∫⁻ xy, ENNReal.ofReal |xy.1-xy.2| ∂(p.law : Measure (ℝ × ℝ))

def fieldWassersteinOne (μ ν : ProbabilityMeasure ℝ) : ℝ≥0∞ :=
  ⨅ p : FieldLawCoupling μ ν, p.cost

def scaledSpectralLaw (ν : ProbabilityMeasure ℝ) (β : ℝ) : ProbabilityMeasure ℝ :=
  ν.map (fun x => β*x)

def groundStateEnergy {N : ℕ} (eig : Fin N → ℝ) (U : Rotation N) : ℝ :=
  (N : ℝ)⁻¹ * Finset.univ.sup' Finset.univ_nonempty (rotatedEnergy eig U)

def thermalVariationalValue (ν : ProbabilityMeasure ℝ) (b β : ℝ) : ℝ :=
  (variationalFunctional (measureR (scaledSpectralLaw ν β : Measure ℝ) (β*b))).toReal/β

abbrev FieldSpectralData (N : ℕ) := (Fin N → ℝ) × (Fin N → ℝ)

def dataPhysicalPressure {N : ℕ} (d : FieldSpectralData N) (U : Orthogonal N) : ℝ :=
  rotatedPressure d.1 (matrixRotation U⁻¹) d.2

def ConditionalFieldOrbitLaw {Ω : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Ω] {N : ℕ}
    (P : Measure Ω) (d : Ω → FieldSpectralData N) (pressure : Ω → ℝ)
    (H : Measure (Orthogonal N)) : Prop :=
  P.map (fun ω => (d ω,pressure ω)) =
    ((P.map d).prod H).map (fun z => (z.1,dataPhysicalPressure z.1 z.2))

def spectralExcess {N : ℕ} (eig : Fin N → ℝ) (a b : ℝ) : ℝ :=
  (insert 0 (Finset.univ.image (fun i => max (a-eig i) (eig i-b)))).max'
    (Finset.insert_nonempty 0 _)

run_cmd Lean.modifyEnv fun env => Lean.Meta.auxLemmasExt.setState env {}

@[irreducible] def gaussianPatternCount (α : ℝ) (N : ℕ) : ℕ := ⌊α*N⌋₊

def marchenkoPasturA (α : ℝ) : ℝ := (1-Real.sqrt α)^2

def marchenkoPasturB (α : ℝ) : ℝ := (1+Real.sqrt α)^2

def marchenkoPasturLower (α : ℝ) : ℝ := if α ≤ 1 then 0 else marchenkoPasturA α

def marchenkoPasturMeasure (α : ℝ) : Measure ℝ :=
  ENNReal.ofReal (max (1-α) 0) • Measure.dirac 0 +
    volume.withDensity ((Icc (marchenkoPasturA α) (marchenkoPasturB α)).indicator
      (fun x => ENNReal.ofReal
        (Real.sqrt ((marchenkoPasturB α-x)*(x-marchenkoPasturA α))/(2*Real.pi*x))))

def gaussianPatternLimit (α c : ℝ) : ℝ :=
  (variationalFunctional (measureR ((marchenkoPasturMeasure α).map (fun x => c*x))
    (max (c*marchenkoPasturLower α) (c*marchenkoPasturB α)))).toReal

run_cmd Lean.modifyEnv fun env => Lean.Meta.auxLemmasExt.setState env {}

def gaussianPatternArray {N m : ℕ} (z : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin N × Fin m)) :
    Matrix (Fin N) (Fin m) ℝ := fun i j => z (i,j)

def gaussianPatternSum {N m : ℕ} (z : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin N × Fin m)) (σ : Spin N) :
    EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin m) :=
  WithLp.toLp 2 ((gaussianPatternArray z)ᵀ *ᵥ (fun i => spinValue (σ i)))

def gaussianPatternPressure {N m : ℕ} (c : ℝ)
    (z : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin N × Fin m)) : ℝ :=
  (N : ℝ)⁻¹*logPartition (fun σ : Spin N => c/(2*N)*‖gaussianPatternSum z σ‖^2)

run_cmd Lean.modifyEnv fun env => Lean.Meta.auxLemmasExt.setState env {}

def gaussianPatternGroundEnergy {N m : ℕ} (ε : ℝ)
    (z : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin N × Fin m)) : ℝ :=
  (N : ℝ)⁻¹*Finset.univ.sup' Finset.univ_nonempty
    (fun σ : Spin N => ε/(2*N)*‖gaussianPatternSum z σ‖^2)

run_cmd Lean.modifyEnv fun env => Lean.Meta.auxLemmasExt.setState env {}

def gaussianPatternColumn {N m : ℕ} (z : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin N × Fin m))
    (i : Fin N) : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin m) := WithLp.toLp 2 (fun j => z (i,j))

def gaussianPatternOperator {N m : ℕ} (z : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin N × Fin m)) :
    (Fin N → ℝ) →L[ℝ] EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin m) :=
  (PiLp.continuousLinearEquiv 2 ℝ (fun _ : Fin m => ℝ)).symm.toContinuousLinearMap.comp
    ((gaussianPatternArray z)ᵀ.mulVecLin.toContinuousLinearMap)

theorem limiting_pressure_of_extreme_limits_unconditional :
  ∀ {Ω : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Ω] (P : Measure Ω) [IsProbabilityMeasure P]
    (U : (N : ℕ) → Ω → Orthogonal N) (_hU : ∀ N, Measurable (U N))
    (_hHaar : ∀ N, (P.map (U N)).IsMulRightInvariant)
    (eig : (N : ℕ) → Fin N → ℝ) (ν : ProbabilityMeasure ℝ) (a b : ℝ)
    (_hcompact : IsCompact (ν : Measure ℝ).support)
    (_hbound : (ν : Measure ℝ).support ⊆ Icc a b)
    (_ha : a∈(ν : Measure ℝ).support) (_hb : b∈(ν : Measure ℝ).support)
    (_hmin : Tendsto (fun k => spectralMinimum (eig (k+1))) atTop (𝓝 a))
    (_hmax : Tendsto (fun k => spectralMaximum (eig (k+1))) atTop (𝓝 b))
    (_hweak : Tendsto (fun k => empiricalSpectralLaw (Nat.succ_pos k) (eig (k+1)))
      atTop (𝓝 ν)),
    Tendsto (fun N => ∫ ω, rotatedPressure (eig N) (matrixRotation (U N ω)⁻¹) (fun _ => 0) ∂P)
      atTop (𝓝 (variationalFunctional (measureR (ν : Measure ℝ) b)).toReal) ∧
    ∀ᵐ ω ∂P, Tendsto (fun N => rotatedPressure (eig N) (matrixRotation (U N ω)⁻¹) (fun _ => 0))
      atTop (𝓝 (variationalFunctional (measureR (ν : Measure ℝ) b)).toReal) := by
  sorry

theorem limiting_wasserstein_field_pressure_of_extreme_limits_unconditional :
  ∀ {Ω : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Ω] (P : Measure Ω) [IsProbabilityMeasure P]
    (U : (N : ℕ) → Ω → Orthogonal N) (_hU : ∀ N, Measurable (U N))
    (_hHaar : ∀ N, (P.map (U N)).IsMulRightInvariant)
    (eig : (N : ℕ) → Fin N → ℝ) (ν : ProbabilityMeasure ℝ) (a b : ℝ)
    (_hcompact : IsCompact (ν : Measure ℝ).support)
    (_hbound : (ν : Measure ℝ).support ⊆ Icc a b)
    (_ha : a∈(ν : Measure ℝ).support) (_hb : b∈(ν : Measure ℝ).support)
    (_hmin : Tendsto (fun k => spectralMinimum (eig (k+1))) atTop (𝓝 a))
    (_hmax : Tendsto (fun k => spectralMaximum (eig (k+1))) atTop (𝓝 b))
    (_hweak : Tendsto (fun k => empiricalSpectralLaw (Nat.succ_pos k) (eig (k+1)))
      atTop (𝓝 ν))
    (field : (N : ℕ) → Fin N → ℝ) (ξ : ProbabilityMeasure ℝ)
    (_hξ : Integrable (fun x : ℝ => x) (ξ : Measure ℝ))
    (_hw : Tendsto (fun k => fieldWassersteinOne (empiricalSpectralLaw (Nat.succ_pos k) (field (k+1))) ξ)
      atTop (𝓝 0)),
    Tendsto (fun N => ∫ ω, rotatedPressure (eig N) (matrixRotation (U N ω)⁻¹) (field N) ∂P)
      atTop (𝓝 (magneticFieldFunctional (ν : Measure ℝ) b ξ)) ∧
    ∀ᵐ ω ∂P, Tendsto (fun N => rotatedPressure (eig N) (matrixRotation (U N ω)⁻¹) (field N))
      atTop (𝓝 (magneticFieldFunctional (ν : Measure ℝ) b ξ)) := by
  sorry

theorem positive_temperature_pressure_unconditional :
  ∀ {Ω : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Ω] (P : Measure Ω) [IsProbabilityMeasure P]
    (U : (N : ℕ) → Ω → Orthogonal N) (_hU : ∀ N, Measurable (U N))
    (_hHaar : ∀ N, (P.map (U N)).IsMulRightInvariant)
    (eig : (N : ℕ) → Fin N → ℝ) (ν : ProbabilityMeasure ℝ) (a b : ℝ)
    (_hbound : (ν : Measure ℝ).support ⊆ Icc a b)
    (_ha : a∈(ν : Measure ℝ).support) (_hb : b∈(ν : Measure ℝ).support)
    (_hno : ∀ ε : ℝ, 0 < ε → ∀ᶠ N in atTop, ∀ i, a-ε ≤ eig N i ∧ eig N i ≤ b+ε)
    (β : ℝ) (_hβ : 0 < β)
    (_hweak : Tendsto (fun k => empiricalSpectralLaw (Nat.succ_pos k) (eig (k+1)))
      atTop (𝓝 ν)),
    Tendsto (fun N => ∫ ω, rotatedPressure (fun i => β*eig N i) (matrixRotation (U N ω)⁻¹) (fun _ => 0) ∂P)
      atTop (𝓝 (variationalFunctional (measureR (scaledSpectralLaw ν β : Measure ℝ) (β*b))).toReal) ∧
    ∀ᵐ ω ∂P, Tendsto (fun N => rotatedPressure (fun i => β*eig N i) (matrixRotation (U N ω)⁻¹) (fun _ => 0))
      atTop (𝓝 (variationalFunctional (measureR (scaledSpectralLaw ν β : Measure ℝ) (β*b))).toReal) := by
  sorry

theorem ground_state_limit_unconditional :
  ∀ {Ω : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Ω] (P : Measure Ω) [IsProbabilityMeasure P]
    (U : (N : ℕ) → Ω → Orthogonal N) (_hU : ∀ N, Measurable (U N))
    (_hHaar : ∀ N, (P.map (U N)).IsMulRightInvariant)
    (eig : (N : ℕ) → Fin N → ℝ) (ν : ProbabilityMeasure ℝ) (a b : ℝ)
    (_hbound : (ν : Measure ℝ).support ⊆ Icc a b)
    (_ha : a∈(ν : Measure ℝ).support) (_hb : b∈(ν : Measure ℝ).support)
    (_hno : ∀ ε : ℝ, 0 < ε → ∀ᶠ N in atTop, ∀ i, a-ε ≤ eig N i ∧ eig N i ≤ b+ε)
    (_hweak : Tendsto (fun k => empiricalSpectralLaw (Nat.succ_pos k) (eig (k+1)))
      atTop (𝓝 ν)),
    ∃ M : ℝ,
      Tendsto (fun N => ∫ ω, groundStateEnergy (eig N) (matrixRotation (U N ω)⁻¹) ∂P)
        atTop (𝓝 M) ∧
      (∀ᵐ ω ∂P, Tendsto (fun N => groundStateEnergy (eig N) (matrixRotation (U N ω)⁻¹))
        atTop (𝓝 M)) ∧
      Tendsto (thermalVariationalValue ν b) atTop (𝓝 M) := by
  sorry

theorem random_pressure_tendsto_in_measure_unconditional
    {Ω : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Ω] (P : Measure Ω) [IsProbabilityMeasure P]
    (eig : (N : ℕ) → Ω → Fin N → ℝ) (Y : ℕ → Ω → ℝ)
    (heig : ∀ N, Measurable (eig N)) (hY : ∀ N, Measurable (Y N))
    (H : (N : ℕ) → Measure (Orthogonal N)) [∀ N, IsProbabilityMeasure (H N)]
    [∀ N, (H N).IsMulRightInvariant]
    (hlaw : ∀ N, ConditionalFieldOrbitLaw P (fun ω => (eig N ω,fun _ => 0)) (Y N) (H N))
    (ν : ProbabilityMeasure ℝ) (a b : ℝ)
    (hcompact : IsCompact (ν : Measure ℝ).support)
    (hbound : (ν : Measure ℝ).support ⊆ Icc a b)
    (ha : a∈(ν : Measure ℝ).support) (hb : b∈(ν : Measure ℝ).support)
    (hweak : TendstoInMeasure P (fun k ω => LevyProkhorov.ofMeasure
      (empiricalSpectralLaw (Nat.succ_pos k) (eig (k+1) ω))) atTop
      (fun _ => LevyProkhorov.ofMeasure ν))
    (hexcess : TendstoInMeasure P (fun k ω => spectralExcess (eig (k+1) ω) a b)
      atTop (fun _ => 0)) :
    TendstoInMeasure P (fun k => Y (k+1)) atTop
      (fun _ => (variationalFunctional (measureR (ν : Measure ℝ) b)).toReal) := by
  sorry

theorem random_pressure_limit_unconditional :
  ∀ {Ω : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Ω] (P : Measure Ω) [IsProbabilityMeasure P]
    (eig : (N : ℕ) → Ω → Fin N → ℝ) (Y : ℕ → Ω → ℝ)
    (_heig : ∀ N, Measurable (eig N)) (_hY : ∀ N, Measurable (Y N))
    (H : (N : ℕ) → Measure (Orthogonal N)) [∀ N, IsProbabilityMeasure (H N)]
    [∀ N, (H N).IsMulRightInvariant]
    (_hlaw : ∀ N, ConditionalFieldOrbitLaw P (fun ω => (eig N ω,fun _ => 0)) (Y N) (H N))
    (ν : ProbabilityMeasure ℝ) (a b : ℝ)
    (_hcompact : IsCompact (ν : Measure ℝ).support)
    (_hbound : (ν : Measure ℝ).support ⊆ Icc a b)
    (_ha : a∈(ν : Measure ℝ).support) (_hb : b∈(ν : Measure ℝ).support)
    (_hweak : TendstoInMeasure P (fun k ω => LevyProkhorov.ofMeasure
      (empiricalSpectralLaw (Nat.succ_pos k) (eig (k+1) ω))) atTop
      (fun _ => LevyProkhorov.ofMeasure ν))
    (_hexcess : TendstoInMeasure P (fun k ω => spectralExcess (eig (k+1) ω) a b)
      atTop (fun _ => 0))
    (_hUI : UniformIntegrable (fun k => Y (k+1)) 1 P),
    TendstoInMeasure P (fun k => Y (k+1)) atTop
      (fun _ => (variationalFunctional (measureR (ν : Measure ℝ) b)).toReal) ∧
    Tendsto (fun k => eLpNorm (fun ω => Y (k+1) ω-
      (variationalFunctional (measureR (ν : Measure ℝ) b)).toReal) 1 P) atTop (𝓝 0) ∧
    Tendsto (fun k => ∫ ω, Y (k+1) ω ∂P) atTop
      (𝓝 (variationalFunctional (measureR (ν : Measure ℝ) b)).toReal) := by
  sorry

theorem random_pressure_tendsto_ae_unconditional :
  ∀ {Ω : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Ω] (P : Measure Ω) [IsProbabilityMeasure P]
    (eig : (N : ℕ) → Ω → Fin N → ℝ) (Y : ℕ → Ω → ℝ)
    (_heig : ∀ N, Measurable (eig N)) (_hY : ∀ N, Measurable (Y N))
    (H : (N : ℕ) → Measure (Orthogonal N)) [∀ N, IsProbabilityMeasure (H N)]
    [∀ N, (H N).IsMulRightInvariant]
    (_hlaw : ∀ N, ConditionalFieldOrbitLaw P (fun ω => (eig N ω,fun _ => 0)) (Y N) (H N))
    (ν : ProbabilityMeasure ℝ) (a b : ℝ)
    (_hcompact : IsCompact (ν : Measure ℝ).support)
    (_hbound : (ν : Measure ℝ).support ⊆ Icc a b)
    (_ha : a∈(ν : Measure ℝ).support) (_hb : b∈(ν : Measure ℝ).support)
    (_hno : ∀ᵐ ω ∂P, ∀ ε : ℝ, 0 < ε → ∀ᶠ N in atTop,
      ∀ i, a-ε ≤ eig N ω i ∧ eig N ω i ≤ b+ε)
    (_hweak : ∀ᵐ ω ∂P, Tendsto (fun k =>
      empiricalSpectralLaw (Nat.succ_pos k) (eig (k+1) ω)) atTop (𝓝 ν)),
    ∀ᵐ ω ∂P, Tendsto (fun N => Y N ω) atTop
      (𝓝 (variationalFunctional (measureR (ν : Measure ℝ) b)).toReal) := by
  sorry

theorem random_field_pressure_tendsto_ae_unconditional :
  ∀ {Ω : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Ω] (P : Measure Ω) [IsProbabilityMeasure P]
    (d : (N : ℕ) → Ω → FieldSpectralData N) (Y : ℕ → Ω → ℝ)
    (_hd : ∀ N, Measurable (d N)) (_hY : ∀ N, Measurable (Y N))
    (H : (N : ℕ) → Measure (Orthogonal N)) [∀ N, IsProbabilityMeasure (H N)]
    [∀ N, (H N).IsMulRightInvariant]
    (_hlaw : ∀ N, ConditionalFieldOrbitLaw P (d N) (Y N) (H N))
    (ν : ProbabilityMeasure ℝ) (a b : ℝ)
    (_hcompact : IsCompact (ν : Measure ℝ).support)
    (_hbound : (ν : Measure ℝ).support ⊆ Icc a b)
    (_ha : a∈(ν : Measure ℝ).support) (_hb : b∈(ν : Measure ℝ).support)
    (_hno : ∀ᵐ ω ∂P, ∀ ε : ℝ, 0 < ε → ∀ᶠ N in atTop,
      ∀ i, a-ε ≤ (d N ω).1 i ∧ (d N ω).1 i ≤ b+ε)
    (_hweak : ∀ᵐ ω ∂P, Tendsto (fun k =>
      empiricalSpectralLaw (Nat.succ_pos k) (d (k+1) ω).1) atTop (𝓝 ν))
    (ξ : ProbabilityMeasure ℝ) (_hξ : Integrable (fun x : ℝ => x) (ξ : Measure ℝ))
    (_hw : ∀ᵐ ω ∂P, Tendsto (fun k => fieldWassersteinOne
      (empiricalSpectralLaw (Nat.succ_pos k) (d (k+1) ω).2) ξ) atTop (𝓝 0)),
    ∀ᵐ ω ∂P, Tendsto (fun N => Y N ω) atTop
      (𝓝 (magneticFieldFunctional (ν : Measure ℝ) b ξ)) := by
  sorry

theorem gaussianPattern_pressure_limit_unconditional :
  ∀ (α : ℝ) (_hα : 0 < α)
    {Ω : Type u} [MeasurableSpace Ω] (P : Measure Ω) [IsProbabilityMeasure P]
    (Z : (N : ℕ) → Ω → EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin N × Fin (gaussianPatternCount α N)))
    (_hZ : ∀ N, Measurable (Z N)) (_hlaw : ∀ N, HasLaw (Z N) (stdGaussian _) P)
    (c : ℝ),
    TendstoInMeasure P (fun k ω => gaussianPatternPressure (N := k+1) (m := gaussianPatternCount α (k+1)) c (Z (k+1) ω)) atTop
      (fun _ => gaussianPatternLimit α c) ∧
    Tendsto (fun k => eLpNorm (fun ω => gaussianPatternPressure (N := k+1) (m := gaussianPatternCount α (k+1)) c (Z (k+1) ω)-
      gaussianPatternLimit α c) 1 P) atTop (𝓝 0) ∧
    Tendsto (fun k => ∫ ω, gaussianPatternPressure (N := k+1) (m := gaussianPatternCount α (k+1)) c (Z (k+1) ω) ∂P) atTop
      (𝓝 (gaussianPatternLimit α c)) := by
  sorry

theorem gaussianPattern_ground_limit_unconditional :
  ∀ (α : ℝ) (_hα : 0 < α)
    {Ω : Type u} [MeasurableSpace Ω] (P : Measure Ω) [IsProbabilityMeasure P]
    (Z : (N : ℕ) → Ω → EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin N × Fin (gaussianPatternCount α N)))
    (_hZ : ∀ N, Measurable (Z N)) (_hlaw : ∀ N, HasLaw (Z N) (stdGaussian _) P)
    (ε : ℝ),
    ∃ e : ℝ,
      TendstoInMeasure P (fun k ω => gaussianPatternGroundEnergy ε (Z (k+1) ω)) atTop (fun _ => e) ∧
      Tendsto (fun k => eLpNorm (fun ω => gaussianPatternGroundEnergy ε (Z (k+1) ω)-e) 1 P)
        atTop (𝓝 0) ∧
      Tendsto (fun k => ∫ ω, gaussianPatternGroundEnergy ε (Z (k+1) ω) ∂P) atTop (𝓝 e) ∧
      Tendsto (fun β => gaussianPatternLimit α (ε*β)/β) atTop (𝓝 e) := by
  sorry

theorem gaussianPattern_norm_limits_unconditional :
  ∀ (α : ℝ) (_hα : 0 < α)
    {Ω : Type u} [MeasurableSpace Ω] (P : Measure Ω) [IsProbabilityMeasure P]
    (Z : (N : ℕ) → Ω → EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin N × Fin (gaussianPatternCount α N)))
    (_hZ : ∀ N, Measurable (Z N)) (_hlaw : ∀ N, HasLaw (Z N) (stdGaussian _) P),
    ∃ eplus eminus : ℝ, 0 ≤ eplus ∧ eminus ≤ 0 ∧
      Tendsto (fun β => gaussianPatternLimit α β/β) atTop (𝓝 eplus) ∧
      Tendsto (fun β => gaussianPatternLimit α (-β)/β) atTop (𝓝 eminus) ∧
      (TendstoInMeasure P (fun k ω => ‖gaussianPatternOperator (Z (k+1) ω)‖/(k+1)) atTop
        (fun _ => Real.sqrt (2*eplus)) ∧
       Tendsto (fun k => eLpNorm (fun ω => ‖gaussianPatternOperator (Z (k+1) ω)‖/(k+1)-
        Real.sqrt (2*eplus)) 1 P) atTop (𝓝 0)) ∧
      (TendstoInMeasure P (fun k ω =>
        (Finset.univ.inf' Finset.univ_nonempty (fun σ : Spin (k+1) => ‖gaussianPatternSum (Z (k+1) ω) σ‖))/(k+1))
        atTop (fun _ => Real.sqrt (-2*eminus)) ∧
       Tendsto (fun k => eLpNorm (fun ω =>
        (Finset.univ.inf' Finset.univ_nonempty (fun σ : Spin (k+1) => ‖gaussianPatternSum (Z (k+1) ω) σ‖))/(k+1)-
          Real.sqrt (-2*eminus)) 1 P) atTop (𝓝 0)) := by
  sorry

end InvariantIsing

end

end OAI

    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "magneticEntropyFunctional",
      "statement_lean": "def magneticEntropyFunctional {A : Type*} [Fintype A] (γ m : A → ℝ)\n    (p : OverlapPath) : EReal :=\n  ⨆ h : FieldStep, ((magneticGroupValue γ m h + fieldPairing p h / 2 : ℝ) : EReal)"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "magneticVariationalFunctional",
      "statement_lean": "def magneticVariationalFunctional {A : Type*} [Fintype A] (R : ℝ → ℝ) (γ m : A → ℝ) : EReal :=\n  ⨅ p : OverlapPath, magneticEntropyFunctional γ m p + (spectralFunctional R p : EReal)"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "RationalMagnetization",
      "statement_lean": "def RationalMagnetization (A : Type*) := {m : A → ℚ // ∀ a, |(m a : ℝ)| < 1}"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "finiteMagneticFunctional",
      "statement_lean": "def finiteMagneticFunctional {A : Type*} [Fintype A] (R : ℝ → ℝ) (γ c : A → ℝ) : EReal :=\n  ⨆ m : RationalMagnetization A,\n    ((∑ a, γ a * c a * (m.val a : ℝ) : ℝ) : EReal) +\n      magneticVariationalFunctional R γ (fun a => (m.val a : ℝ))"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "finiteIndexWeight",
      "statement_lean": "def finiteIndexWeight {Ω A : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Ω]\n    (P : Measure Ω) (g : Ω → A) (a : A) : ℝ := P.real (g ⁻¹' {a})"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "simpleFieldIndex",
      "statement_lean": "def simpleFieldIndex {Ω : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Ω] (s : SimpleFunc Ω ℝ) (x : Ω) : s.range :=\n  ⟨s x,s.mem_range_self x⟩"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "simpleFieldWeight",
      "statement_lean": "def simpleFieldWeight {Ω : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Ω] (P : Measure Ω) (s : SimpleFunc Ω ℝ) :=\n  finiteIndexWeight P (simpleFieldIndex s)"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "abbrev",
      "name": "SimpleFieldPositive",
      "statement_lean": "abbrev SimpleFieldPositive {Ω : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Ω] (P : Measure Ω) (s : SimpleFunc Ω ℝ) :=\n  {i : s.range // 0 < simpleFieldWeight P s i}"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "simpleFieldValue",
      "statement_lean": "def simpleFieldValue {Ω : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Ω]\n    (P : Measure Ω) [IsProbabilityMeasure P] (s : SimpleFunc Ω ℝ) (R : ℝ → ℝ) : ℝ :=\n  (finiteMagneticFunctional R (fun i : SimpleFieldPositive P s => simpleFieldWeight P s i)\n    (fun i => i.val.val)).toReal"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "fieldSimpleApproximation",
      "statement_lean": "def fieldSimpleApproximation (n : ℕ) : SimpleFunc ℝ ℝ :=\n  SimpleFunc.approxOn id measurable_id (range (id : ℝ → ℝ) ∪ {0}) 0 (by simp) n"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "magneticFieldFunctional",
      "statement_lean": "def magneticFieldFunctional (spectral : Measure ℝ) (edge : ℝ) (field : ProbabilityMeasure ℝ) : ℝ :=\n  limUnder atTop (fun n => simpleFieldValue (field : Measure ℝ) (fieldSimpleApproximation n)\n    (measureR spectral edge))"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "structure",
      "name": "FieldLawCoupling",
      "statement_lean": "structure FieldLawCoupling (μ ν : ProbabilityMeasure ℝ) where\n  law : ProbabilityMeasure (ℝ × ℝ)\n  fst : (law : Measure (ℝ × ℝ)).map Prod.fst = (μ : Measure ℝ)\n  snd : (law : Measure (ℝ × ℝ)).map Prod.snd = (ν : Measure ℝ)"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "FieldLawCoupling.cost",
      "statement_lean": "def FieldLawCoupling.cost {μ ν : ProbabilityMeasure ℝ} (p : FieldLawCoupling μ ν) : ℝ≥0∞ :=\n  ∫⁻ xy, ENNReal.ofReal |xy.1-xy.2| ∂(p.law : Measure (ℝ × ℝ))"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "fieldWassersteinOne",
      "statement_lean": "def fieldWassersteinOne (μ ν : ProbabilityMeasure ℝ) : ℝ≥0∞ :=\n  ⨅ p : FieldLawCoupling μ ν, p.cost"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "scaledSpectralLaw",
      "statement_lean": "def scaledSpectralLaw (ν : ProbabilityMeasure ℝ) (β : ℝ) : ProbabilityMeasure ℝ :=\n  ν.map (fun x => β*x)"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "groundStateEnergy",
      "statement_lean": "def groundStateEnergy {N : ℕ} (eig : Fin N → ℝ) (U : Rotation N) : ℝ :=\n  (N : ℝ)⁻¹ * Finset.univ.sup' Finset.univ_nonempty (rotatedEnergy eig U)"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "thermalVariationalValue",
      "statement_lean": "def thermalVariationalValue (ν : ProbabilityMeasure ℝ) (b β : ℝ) : ℝ :=\n  (variationalFunctional (measureR (scaledSpectralLaw ν β : Measure ℝ) (β*b))).toReal/β"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "abbrev",
      "name": "FieldSpectralData",
      "statement_lean": "abbrev FieldSpectralData (N : ℕ) := (Fin N → ℝ) × (Fin N → ℝ)"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "dataPhysicalPressure",
      "statement_lean": "def dataPhysicalPressure {N : ℕ} (d : FieldSpectralData N) (U : Orthogonal N) : ℝ :=\n  rotatedPressure d.1 (matrixRotation U⁻¹) d.2"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "ConditionalFieldOrbitLaw",
      "statement_lean": "def ConditionalFieldOrbitLaw {Ω : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Ω] {N : ℕ}\n    (P : Measure Ω) (d : Ω → FieldSpectralData N) (pressure : Ω → ℝ)\n    (H : Measure (Orthogonal N)) : Prop :=\n  P.map (fun ω => (d ω,pressure ω)) =\n    ((P.map d).prod H).map (fun z => (z.1,dataPhysicalPressure z.1 z.2))"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "spectralExcess",
      "statement_lean": "def spectralExcess {N : ℕ} (eig : Fin N → ℝ) (a b : ℝ) : ℝ :=\n  (insert 0 (Finset.univ.image (fun i => max (a-eig i) (eig i-b)))).max'\n    (Finset.insert_nonempty 0 _)\n\nrun_cmd Lean.modifyEnv fun env => Lean.Meta.auxLemmasExt.setState env {}"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "gaussianPatternCount",
      "statement_lean": "@[irreducible] def gaussianPatternCount (α : ℝ) (N : ℕ) : ℕ := ⌊α*N⌋₊"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "marchenkoPasturA",
      "statement_lean": "def marchenkoPasturA (α : ℝ) : ℝ := (1-Real.sqrt α)^2"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "marchenkoPasturB",
      "statement_lean": "def marchenkoPasturB (α : ℝ) : ℝ := (1+Real.sqrt α)^2"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "marchenkoPasturLower",
      "statement_lean": "def marchenkoPasturLower (α : ℝ) : ℝ := if α ≤ 1 then 0 else marchenkoPasturA α"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "marchenkoPasturMeasure",
      "statement_lean": "def marchenkoPasturMeasure (α : ℝ) : Measure ℝ :=\n  ENNReal.ofReal (max (1-α) 0) • Measure.dirac 0 +\n    volume.withDensity ((Icc (marchenkoPasturA α) (marchenkoPasturB α)).indicator\n      (fun x => ENNReal.ofReal\n        (Real.sqrt ((marchenkoPasturB α-x)*(x-marchenkoPasturA α))/(2*Real.pi*x))))"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "gaussianPatternLimit",
      "statement_lean": "def gaussianPatternLimit (α c : ℝ) : ℝ :=\n  (variationalFunctional (measureR ((marchenkoPasturMeasure α).map (fun x => c*x))\n    (max (c*marchenkoPasturLower α) (c*marchenkoPasturB α)))).toReal\n\nrun_cmd Lean.modifyEnv fun env => Lean.Meta.auxLemmasExt.setState env {}"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "gaussianPatternArray",
      "statement_lean": "def gaussianPatternArray {N m : ℕ} (z : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin N × Fin m)) :\n    Matrix (Fin N) (Fin m) ℝ := fun i j => z (i,j)"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "gaussianPatternSum",
      "statement_lean": "def gaussianPatternSum {N m : ℕ} (z : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin N × Fin m)) (σ : Spin N) :\n    EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin m) :=\n  WithLp.toLp 2 ((gaussianPatternArray z)ᵀ *ᵥ (fun i => spinValue (σ i)))"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "gaussianPatternPressure",
      "statement_lean": "def gaussianPatternPressure {N m : ℕ} (c : ℝ)\n    (z : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin N × Fin m)) : ℝ :=\n  (N : ℝ)⁻¹*logPartition (fun σ : Spin N => c/(2*N)*‖gaussianPatternSum z σ‖^2)\n\nrun_cmd Lean.modifyEnv fun env => Lean.Meta.auxLemmasExt.setState env {}"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "gaussianPatternGroundEnergy",
      "statement_lean": "def gaussianPatternGroundEnergy {N m : ℕ} (ε : ℝ)\n    (z : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin N × Fin m)) : ℝ :=\n  (N : ℝ)⁻¹*Finset.univ.sup' Finset.univ_nonempty\n    (fun σ : Spin N => ε/(2*N)*‖gaussianPatternSum z σ‖^2)\n\nrun_cmd Lean.modifyEnv fun env => Lean.Meta.auxLemmasExt.setState env {}"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "gaussianPatternColumn",
      "statement_lean": "def gaussianPatternColumn {N m : ℕ} (z : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin N × Fin m))\n    (i : Fin N) : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin m) := WithLp.toLp 2 (fun j => z (i,j))"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "gaussianPatternOperator",
      "statement_lean": "def gaussianPatternOperator {N m : ℕ} (z : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin N × Fin m)) :\n    (Fin N → ℝ) →L[ℝ] EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin m) :=\n  (PiLp.continuousLinearEquiv 2 ℝ (fun _ : Fin m => ℝ)).symm.toContinuousLinearMap.comp\n    ((gaussianPatternArray z)ᵀ.mulVecLin.toContinuousLinearMap)"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "theorem",
      "name": "limiting_pressure_of_extreme_limits_unconditional",
      "statement_lean": "theorem limiting_pressure_of_extreme_limits_unconditional :\n  ∀ {Ω : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Ω] (P : Measure Ω) [IsProbabilityMeasure P]\n    (U : (N : ℕ) → Ω → Orthogonal N) (_hU : ∀ N, Measurable (U N))\n    (_hHaar : ∀ N, (P.map (U N)).IsMulRightInvariant)\n    (eig : (N : ℕ) → Fin N → ℝ) (ν : ProbabilityMeasure ℝ) (a b : ℝ)\n    (_hcompact : IsCompact (ν : Measure ℝ).support)\n    (_hbound : (ν : Measure ℝ).support ⊆ Icc a b)\n    (_ha : a∈(ν : Measure ℝ).support) (_hb : b∈(ν : Measure ℝ).support)\n    (_hmin : Tendsto (fun k => spectralMinimum (eig (k+1))) atTop (𝓝 a))\n    (_hmax : Tendsto (fun k => spectralMaximum (eig (k+1))) atTop (𝓝 b))\n    (_hweak : Tendsto (fun k => empiricalSpectralLaw (Nat.succ_pos k) (eig (k+1)))\n      atTop (𝓝 ν)),\n    Tendsto (fun N => ∫ ω, rotatedPressure (eig N) (matrixRotation (U N ω)⁻¹) (fun _ => 0) ∂P)\n      atTop (𝓝 (variationalFunctional (measureR (ν : Measure ℝ) b)).toReal) ∧\n    ∀ᵐ ω ∂P, Tendsto (fun N => rotatedPressure (eig N) (matrixRotation (U N ω)⁻¹) (fun _ => 0))\n      atTop (𝓝 (variationalFunctional (measureR (ν : Measure ℝ) b)).toReal) := by\n  sorry"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "theorem",
      "name": "limiting_wasserstein_field_pressure_of_extreme_limits_unconditional",
      "statement_lean": "theorem limiting_wasserstein_field_pressure_of_extreme_limits_unconditional :\n  ∀ {Ω : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Ω] (P : Measure Ω) [IsProbabilityMeasure P]\n    (U : (N : ℕ) → Ω → Orthogonal N) (_hU : ∀ N, Measurable (U N))\n    (_hHaar : ∀ N, (P.map (U N)).IsMulRightInvariant)\n    (eig : (N : ℕ) → Fin N → ℝ) (ν : ProbabilityMeasure ℝ) (a b : ℝ)\n    (_hcompact : IsCompact (ν : Measure ℝ).support)\n    (_hbound : (ν : Measure ℝ).support ⊆ Icc a b)\n    (_ha : a∈(ν : Measure ℝ).support) (_hb : b∈(ν : Measure ℝ).support)\n    (_hmin : Tendsto (fun k => spectralMinimum (eig (k+1))) atTop (𝓝 a))\n    (_hmax : Tendsto (fun k => spectralMaximum (eig (k+1))) atTop (𝓝 b))\n    (_hweak : Tendsto (fun k => empiricalSpectralLaw (Nat.succ_pos k) (eig (k+1)))\n      atTop (𝓝 ν))\n    (field : (N : ℕ) → Fin N → ℝ) (ξ : ProbabilityMeasure ℝ)\n    (_hξ : Integrable (fun x : ℝ => x) (ξ : Measure ℝ))\n    (_hw : Tendsto (fun k => fieldWassersteinOne (empiricalSpectralLaw (Nat.succ_pos k) (field (k+1))) ξ)\n      atTop (𝓝 0)),\n    Tendsto (fun N => ∫ ω, rotatedPressure (eig N) (matrixRotation (U N ω)⁻¹) (field N) ∂P)\n      atTop (𝓝 (magneticFieldFunctional (ν : Measure ℝ) b ξ)) ∧\n    ∀ᵐ ω ∂P, Tendsto (fun N => rotatedPressure (eig N) (matrixRotation (U N ω)⁻¹) (field N))\n      atTop (𝓝 (magneticFieldFunctional (ν : Measure ℝ) b ξ)) := by\n  sorry"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "theorem",
      "name": "positive_temperature_pressure_unconditional",
      "statement_lean": "theorem positive_temperature_pressure_unconditional :\n  ∀ {Ω : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Ω] (P : Measure Ω) [IsProbabilityMeasure P]\n    (U : (N : ℕ) → Ω → Orthogonal N) (_hU : ∀ N, Measurable (U N))\n    (_hHaar : ∀ N, (P.map (U N)).IsMulRightInvariant)\n    (eig : (N : ℕ) → Fin N → ℝ) (ν : ProbabilityMeasure ℝ) (a b : ℝ)\n    (_hbound : (ν : Measure ℝ).support ⊆ Icc a b)\n    (_ha : a∈(ν : Measure ℝ).support) (_hb : b∈(ν : Measure ℝ).support)\n    (_hno : ∀ ε : ℝ, 0 < ε → ∀ᶠ N in atTop, ∀ i, a-ε ≤ eig N i ∧ eig N i ≤ b+ε)\n    (β : ℝ) (_hβ : 0 < β)\n    (_hweak : Tendsto (fun k => empiricalSpectralLaw (Nat.succ_pos k) (eig (k+1)))\n      atTop (𝓝 ν)),\n    Tendsto (fun N => ∫ ω, rotatedPressure (fun i => β*eig N i) (matrixRotation (U N ω)⁻¹) (fun _ => 0) ∂P)\n      atTop (𝓝 (variationalFunctional (measureR (scaledSpectralLaw ν β : Measure ℝ) (β*b))).toReal) ∧\n    ∀ᵐ ω ∂P, Tendsto (fun N => rotatedPressure (fun i => β*eig N i) (matrixRotation (U N ω)⁻¹) (fun _ => 0))\n      atTop (𝓝 (variationalFunctional (measureR (scaledSpectralLaw ν β : Measure ℝ) (β*b))).toReal) := by\n  sorry"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "theorem",
      "name": "ground_state_limit_unconditional",
      "statement_lean": "theorem ground_state_limit_unconditional :\n  ∀ {Ω : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Ω] (P : Measure Ω) [IsProbabilityMeasure P]\n    (U : (N : ℕ) → Ω → Orthogonal N) (_hU : ∀ N, Measurable (U N))\n    (_hHaar : ∀ N, (P.map (U N)).IsMulRightInvariant)\n    (eig : (N : ℕ) → Fin N → ℝ) (ν : ProbabilityMeasure ℝ) (a b : ℝ)\n    (_hbound : (ν : Measure ℝ).support ⊆ Icc a b)\n    (_ha : a∈(ν : Measure ℝ).support) (_hb : b∈(ν : Measure ℝ).support)\n    (_hno : ∀ ε : ℝ, 0 < ε → ∀ᶠ N in atTop, ∀ i, a-ε ≤ eig N i ∧ eig N i ≤ b+ε)\n    (_hweak : Tendsto (fun k => empiricalSpectralLaw (Nat.succ_pos k) (eig (k+1)))\n      atTop (𝓝 ν)),\n    ∃ M : ℝ,\n      Tendsto (fun N => ∫ ω, groundStateEnergy (eig N) (matrixRotation (U N ω)⁻¹) ∂P)\n        atTop (𝓝 M) ∧\n      (∀ᵐ ω ∂P, Tendsto (fun N => groundStateEnergy (eig N) (matrixRotation (U N ω)⁻¹))\n        atTop (𝓝 M)) ∧\n      Tendsto (thermalVariationalValue ν b) atTop (𝓝 M) := by\n  sorry"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "theorem",
      "name": "random_pressure_tendsto_in_measure_unconditional",
      "statement_lean": "theorem random_pressure_tendsto_in_measure_unconditional\n    {Ω : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Ω] (P : Measure Ω) [IsProbabilityMeasure P]\n    (eig : (N : ℕ) → Ω → Fin N → ℝ) (Y : ℕ → Ω → ℝ)\n    (heig : ∀ N, Measurable (eig N)) (hY : ∀ N, Measurable (Y N))\n    (H : (N : ℕ) → Measure (Orthogonal N)) [∀ N, IsProbabilityMeasure (H N)]\n    [∀ N, (H N).IsMulRightInvariant]\n    (hlaw : ∀ N, ConditionalFieldOrbitLaw P (fun ω => (eig N ω,fun _ => 0)) (Y N) (H N))\n    (ν : ProbabilityMeasure ℝ) (a b : ℝ)\n    (hcompact : IsCompact (ν : Measure ℝ).support)\n    (hbound : (ν : Measure ℝ).support ⊆ Icc a b)\n    (ha : a∈(ν : Measure ℝ).support) (hb : b∈(ν : Measure ℝ).support)\n    (hweak : TendstoInMeasure P (fun k ω => LevyProkhorov.ofMeasure\n      (empiricalSpectralLaw (Nat.succ_pos k) (eig (k+1) ω))) atTop\n      (fun _ => LevyProkhorov.ofMeasure ν))\n    (hexcess : TendstoInMeasure P (fun k ω => spectralExcess (eig (k+1) ω) a b)\n      atTop (fun _ => 0)) :\n    TendstoInMeasure P (fun k => Y (k+1)) atTop\n      (fun _ => (variationalFunctional (measureR (ν : Measure ℝ) b)).toReal) := by\n  sorry"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "theorem",
      "name": "random_pressure_limit_unconditional",
      "statement_lean": "theorem random_pressure_limit_unconditional :\n  ∀ {Ω : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Ω] (P : Measure Ω) [IsProbabilityMeasure P]\n    (eig : (N : ℕ) → Ω → Fin N → ℝ) (Y : ℕ → Ω → ℝ)\n    (_heig : ∀ N, Measurable (eig N)) (_hY : ∀ N, Measurable (Y N))\n    (H : (N : ℕ) → Measure (Orthogonal N)) [∀ N, IsProbabilityMeasure (H N)]\n    [∀ N, (H N).IsMulRightInvariant]\n    (_hlaw : ∀ N, ConditionalFieldOrbitLaw P (fun ω => (eig N ω,fun _ => 0)) (Y N) (H N))\n    (ν : ProbabilityMeasure ℝ) (a b : ℝ)\n    (_hcompact : IsCompact (ν : Measure ℝ).support)\n    (_hbound : (ν : Measure ℝ).support ⊆ Icc a b)\n    (_ha : a∈(ν : Measure ℝ).support) (_hb : b∈(ν : Measure ℝ).support)\n    (_hweak : TendstoInMeasure P (fun k ω => LevyProkhorov.ofMeasure\n      (empiricalSpectralLaw (Nat.succ_pos k) (eig (k+1) ω))) atTop\n      (fun _ => LevyProkhorov.ofMeasure ν))\n    (_hexcess : TendstoInMeasure P (fun k ω => spectralExcess (eig (k+1) ω) a b)\n      atTop (fun _ => 0))\n    (_hUI : UniformIntegrable (fun k => Y (k+1)) 1 P),\n    TendstoInMeasure P (fun k => Y (k+1)) atTop\n      (fun _ => (variationalFunctional (measureR (ν : Measure ℝ) b)).toReal) ∧\n    Tendsto (fun k => eLpNorm (fun ω => Y (k+1) ω-\n      (variationalFunctional (measureR (ν : Measure ℝ) b)).toReal) 1 P) atTop (𝓝 0) ∧\n    Tendsto (fun k => ∫ ω, Y (k+1) ω ∂P) atTop\n      (𝓝 (variationalFunctional (measureR (ν : Measure ℝ) b)).toReal) := by\n  sorry"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "theorem",
      "name": "random_pressure_tendsto_ae_unconditional",
      "statement_lean": "theorem random_pressure_tendsto_ae_unconditional :\n  ∀ {Ω : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Ω] (P : Measure Ω) [IsProbabilityMeasure P]\n    (eig : (N : ℕ) → Ω → Fin N → ℝ) (Y : ℕ → Ω → ℝ)\n    (_heig : ∀ N, Measurable (eig N)) (_hY : ∀ N, Measurable (Y N))\n    (H : (N : ℕ) → Measure (Orthogonal N)) [∀ N, IsProbabilityMeasure (H N)]\n    [∀ N, (H N).IsMulRightInvariant]\n    (_hlaw : ∀ N, ConditionalFieldOrbitLaw P (fun ω => (eig N ω,fun _ => 0)) (Y N) (H N))\n    (ν : ProbabilityMeasure ℝ) (a b : ℝ)\n    (_hcompact : IsCompact (ν : Measure ℝ).support)\n    (_hbound : (ν : Measure ℝ).support ⊆ Icc a b)\n    (_ha : a∈(ν : Measure ℝ).support) (_hb : b∈(ν : Measure ℝ).support)\n    (_hno : ∀ᵐ ω ∂P, ∀ ε : ℝ, 0 < ε → ∀ᶠ N in atTop,\n      ∀ i, a-ε ≤ eig N ω i ∧ eig N ω i ≤ b+ε)\n    (_hweak : ∀ᵐ ω ∂P, Tendsto (fun k =>\n      empiricalSpectralLaw (Nat.succ_pos k) (eig (k+1) ω)) atTop (𝓝 ν)),\n    ∀ᵐ ω ∂P, Tendsto (fun N => Y N ω) atTop\n      (𝓝 (variationalFunctional (measureR (ν : Measure ℝ) b)).toReal) := by\n  sorry"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,

<a id="pm-s7"></a>
## 7. Complete mathematical paper


We determine the limiting pressure of an Ising spin glass with Haar orthogonal eigenvectors, a compact limiting spectral law, and no asymptotic outliers, at every fixed temperature. The pressure converges in expectation and almost surely to a variational formula. We also give the limiting pressure with deterministic external fields whose empirical laws converge in first-moment transport distance, and derive a formula for the zero-field ground-state energy by taking temperature to zero.

<a id="sec:introduction"></a>
### Introduction

The eigenvalues of an interaction matrix describe its quadratic form on Euclidean space, but an Ising spin system samples that form only on the vertices of a cube. Randomizing the eigenvectors by Haar orthogonal conjugation leads to the problem of determining the thermodynamic pressure from the limiting spectral distribution. We solve this problem for every fixed temperature when the limiting spectral law is compactly supported and there are no asymptotic outliers. The formula retains the hierarchy of replica overlaps familiar from the Sherrington–Kirkpatrick model, while the spectral law enters through its edge-extended $R$-transform. The rigorous pressure formulas in <a id="pm-cite-1"></a>[[BhattacharyaSen](#ref-BhattacharyaSen), [FanWu](#ref-FanWu), [Fan2026](#ref-Fan2026)] impose high-temperature conditions.

<a id="pm-h-3"></a>
#### The model and the spectral transform

<a id="eq:pressure"></a>
Let $\Lambda_N=\mathop{\mathrm{diag}}(\lambda_{1,N},\ldots,\lambda_{N,N})$ be deterministic, and let $U_N$ be Haar distributed on the orthogonal group $O(N)$. Set $$P_N=\frac1N\log\left[
 2^{-N}\sum_{\sigma\in\{-1,1\}^N}
 \exp\left\{\frac12\sigma^{\mathsf T}
 U_N^{\mathsf T}\Lambda_NU_N\sigma\right\}\right].
 \label{eq:pressure}$$ Temperature and coupling normalization are absorbed into $\Lambda_N$. Our spin prior is uniform probability, not counting measure.

<a id="eq:spectral-assumptions"></a>
Write $\mu_N=N^{-1}\sum_i\delta_{\lambda_{i,N}}$. Throughout the main theorem, $\mu$ is a compactly supported probability measure on $\mathbb R$, with support edges $\lambda_-\le\lambda_+$, and $$\mu_N\Rightarrow\mu,\qquad
 \liminf_{N\to\infty}\min_i\lambda_{i,N}\ge\lambda_-,\qquad
 \limsup_{N\to\infty}\max_i\lambda_{i,N}\le\lambda_+.
 \label{eq:spectral-assumptions}$$ Weak convergence then forces convergence of both extreme eigenvalues to the corresponding edges. No density or nonzero-variance assumption is imposed on $\mu$.

<a id="eq:R"></a>
For $x>0$, let $b(x)>\lambda_+$ be the unique solution, when it exists, of <a id="eq:resolvent"></a>$$\int\frac{\mu(d\lambda)}{b(x)-\lambda}=x.
 \label{eq:resolvent}$$ If there is no such solution, put $b(x)=\lambda_+$. Define the edge-extended $R$-transform by $$R(x)=b(x)-\frac1x\quad(x>0),\qquad
 R(0)=\int\lambda\,\mu(d\lambda).
 \label{eq:R}$$ Only $0\le x\le1$ is needed in the final formula. The extension at the upper spectral edge is part of the definition; it is not an analytic continuation beyond the inverse-transform range.

<a id="pm-h-4"></a>
#### The variational functional

For spin configurations $\sigma,\tau\in\{-1,1\}^N$, their overlap is $Q(\sigma,\tau)=N^{-1}\sigma\cdot\tau$. Let $\mathcal P$ be the nondecreasing measurable functions $p:(0,1)\to[0,1]$, identified up to null sets. These are the trial overlap quantiles. The nonnegative laws used in the proof come from subsequential limits after small perturbations that leave the limiting pressure unchanged. For $p\in\mathcal P$, put <a id="eq:D"></a>$$D_p(r)=1-r-\int_0^1(p(s)-r)_+\,ds,\qquad 0\le r\le1.
 \label{eq:D}$$ This function is nonnegative and Lipschitz. Its value at $r=1$ is zero.

<a id="eq:S"></a>
<a id="eq:G"></a>
The one-site part of the formula is defined by a finite Gaussian recursion. Its auxiliary paths specify hierarchical Gaussian covariance levels. Let $\mathcal H$ consist of the nonnegative nondecreasing finite step functions on $(0,1)$. Describe $h\in\mathcal H$ by $k$ consecutive intervals of lengths $w_i>0$ and values $h_i$, for $0\le i<k$, and set $$\zeta_i=\sum_{l<i}w_l,\qquad \zeta_k=1,
 \qquad h_{-1}=0.$$ For $a,v\ge0$ and a function $f:\mathbb R\to\mathbb R$, write $$(T_{a,v}f)(z)=
 \begin{cases}
 a^{-1}\log\mathbb E\exp\{a f(z+\sqrt v\,g)\},&a>0,\\
 \mathbb Ef(z+\sqrt v\,g),&a=0,
 \end{cases}
 \qquad g\sim N(0,1).$$ Starting from $F_{k-1}(z)=\log\cosh z$, define successively <a id="eq:field-recursion"></a>$$F_{i-1}=T_{\zeta_i,h_i-h_{i-1}}F_i
 \quad (i=k-1,\ldots,0),\qquad
 \ell(h)=F_{-1}(0)-\frac{h_{k-1}}2.
 \label{eq:field-recursion}$$ Subdividing intervals on which $h$ is constant does not change the value. For $p\in\mathcal P$ and $0\le t\le1$, set $$\begin{aligned}
 S(p)&=\sup_{h\in\mathcal H}
 \left\{\ell(h)+\frac12\int_0^1p(s)h(s)\,ds\right\},
 \label{eq:S}\\
 G_t(p)&=\frac12\int_0^1tR(tD_p(r))\,dr,
 \qquad G_0(p)=0.
 \label{eq:G}
\end{aligned}$$ The supremum is interpreted in the extended real sense. Both $S$ and the class of admissible fields are independent of the spectral measure.

<a id="stmt-thm-main"></a>
<div id="thm:main" class="theorem">

<a id="eq:variational"></a>
**Theorem 1** (Limiting pressure). *Let $\Lambda_N$ be deterministic real diagonal matrices whose empirical spectral measures converge weakly to a compactly supported probability law $\mu$. Assume that their smallest and largest eigenvalues converge to the respective edges of $\mathop{\mathrm{supp}}\mu$.
